Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on membranes structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 276. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 431 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_276(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 276. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^431(S, F) is trivial. QED.
